Perfect! Now I have the full context. Let me break down what each commit does:

## Commit Breakdown

### **Commit 1: Add ExtractAllModifiedFiles** (74e2df1a)
**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/transcript.go`

This adds the core function `ExtractAllModifiedFiles()`:
- Takes `transcriptPath`, `startLine`, and `subagentsDir` as parameters
- **First**: Parses the main transcript and extracts modified files using the existing `ExtractModifiedFiles()` function
- **Then**: Uses `ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs()` to find all subagents spawned via Task tool
- **For each subagent**: Reads their transcript file (`agent-<agentID>.jsonl`) from `subagentsDir` and extracts their modified files
- **Deduplicates** across main agent and all subagents using a map
- **Returns** the deduplicated list of all modified files

Key insight: This is designed to be called with the subagent path structure: `<transcriptDir>/<modelSessionID>/subagents/`

---

### **Commit 2: Update Stop hook** (4630d91e)
**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` (in `commitWithMetadata()`)

The Stop hook now:
1. **Constructs the subagents directory path** from the transcript location:
   ```go
   subagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(transcriptPath), input.SessionID, "subagents")
   ```

2. **Calls `ExtractAllModifiedFiles()`** instead of the old `extractModifiedFiles()`:
   ```go
   modifiedFiles, subagentErr := claudecode.ExtractAllModifiedFiles(
       transcriptPath, transcriptOffset, subagentsDir)
   ```

3. **Falls back gracefully**: If subagent extraction fails, it falls back to the main transcript only

Also fixes a variable shadowing issue where `subagentsDir` was being redeclared and recomputed for the token usage calculation.

---

### **Commit 3: Fix sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript** (664fde3b)
**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` (in `sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript()`)

This function now:
1. **Checks if the agent is Claude Code** and has a transcript:
   ```go
   if state.AgentType == agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode && state.TranscriptPath != "" {
   ```

2. **Extracts subagent modified files** using the same subagent path structure
3. **Compares counts**: If `ExtractAllModifiedFiles()` finds MORE files than the main transcript analysis, it uses those instead
4. **Logs any extraction errors** but doesn't fail — just uses what it has

This is the **critical fix** for the ENT-297 bug: Without this, `sessionHasNewContent()` would return false when only subagents made changes, causing checkpoints to be skipped entirely.

---

### **Commit 4: Add integration test** (b2b0706c)
**Files:** 
- `cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/hooks_test.go` — main test
- `cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/transcript.go` — helper updates
- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/mid_turn_commit_test.go` — unit test

**Test 1: `TestHookRunner_SimulateStop_SubagentOnlyChanges`** (integration test)
- Creates a real session with both main and subagent transcripts
- **Main transcript**: Only contains Task tool call (no Write/Edit)
- **Subagent transcript**: Contains Write tool to `subagent_output.go`
- **File on disk**: Creates the actual file the subagent "wrote"
- Calls `SimulateStop()` and verifies:
  - **Auto-commit strategy**: New commit created on active branch
  - **Manual-commit strategy**: Shadow branch exists, session state has checkpoint count

**Test 2: `TestSessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript_IncludesSubagentFiles`** (unit test)
- Creates a staged file in git index
- Creates main transcript with **only** Task tool call
- Creates subagent transcript with Write tool to the staged file
- Tests `sessionHasNewContent()` returns `true` (should detect the subagent modification)
- Without the fix, this would return `false` and skip the checkpoint

**Test helpers** added:
- `writeJSONLFile()` — write transcript lines to file
- `makeWriteToolLine()` — create Write tool_use JSONL
- `makeEditToolLine()` — create Edit tool_use JSONL
- `makeTaskToolUseLine()` — create Task tool_use JSONL
- `makeTaskResultLine()` — create tool_result with agentId
- Updated `TranscriptBuilder.AddTaskToolResult()` to use correct format: `"agentId: " + agentID`

---

### **Commit 5: Address PR review** (97fb2a99)
Minor cleanups:
- Changed `//nolint:ireturn` comments from generic `"already present in codebase"` to more descriptive comments like `"factory returns interface by design"`

---

## Summary of the Bug Fix

**The Bug (ENT-297):**
When Claude Code spawned subagents via the Task tool that **only modified files** (no direct modifications by the main agent), the checkpoint system would skip creating a checkpoint because:
1. `sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript()` only looked at the main transcript
2. It didn't see any file modifications (they were all in subagent transcripts)
3. It returned `false`, skipping checkpoint creation
4. Result: Subagent work was lost

**The Fix:**
- `ExtractAllModifiedFiles()` reads both main and subagent transcripts
- The Stop hook uses it instead of the old function
- `sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript()` also checks subagent transcripts
- Checkpoints are now created even when only subagents modify files

Perfect! Now I have all the information I need. Let me create a comprehensive summary document.

## Summary: Key Changes in `soph/one-to-one-checkpoints` Branch

Based on my thorough analysis of the current code (rebased on this branch), here are the critical architectural changes:

### 1. SESSION PHASE STATE MACHINE (session/phase.go)

**PHASE SIMPLIFIED (ACTIVE_COMMITTED removed):**
- **Old phases:** ACTIVE, ACTIVE_COMMITTED, IDLE, ENDED
- **New phases:** ACTIVE, IDLE, ENDED

**Backward compatibility:** The `PhaseFromString()` function (line 25-40) handles the migration:
```go
case PhaseActive, "active_committed":
    // "active_committed" was removed with the 1:1 checkpoint model.
    // It previously meant "agent active + commit happened during turn".
    // Normalize to ACTIVE so HandleTurnEnd can finalize any pending checkpoints.
    return PhaseActive
```

**KEY INSIGHT:** This is the core of the "1:1 checkpoint model" - instead of deferring condensation when the agent commits mid-turn (ACTIVE_COMMITTED state), the new model condenses IMMEDIATELY at commit time and carries forward remaining files to a new shadow branch. This enables each commit to get its own checkpoint with 1:1 correspondence.

---

### 2. ACTIONS EMITTED BY STATE MACHINE

**New Action Set (session/phase.go, lines 83-90):**
- `ActionCondense` - Condense immediately
- `ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched` - Condense only if files were touched
- `ActionDiscardIfNoFiles` - Discard empty ENDED sessions
- `ActionWarnStaleSession` - Warn about stale sessions
- `ActionClearEndedAt` - Clear EndedAt when session re-enters
- `ActionUpdateLastInteraction` - Update LastInteractionTime

**ACTIVE → IDLE transition (TurnEnd):** Returns `[ActionUpdateLastInteraction]` only - no more deferral

**ACTIVE + EventGitCommit:** Returns `[ActionCondense, ActionUpdateLastInteraction]` - condense NOW, not later

---

### 3. POSTCOMMIT HOOK FLOW (manual_commit_hooks.go, lines 454-700)

**Dramatic change in strategy:**

**Old approach (with ACTIVE_COMMITTED):**
1. User commits mid-turn → phase becomes ACTIVE_COMMITTED
2. On TurnEnd → THEN condense and finalize

**New approach (1:1 checkpoints):**
1. PostCommit runs state machine transition → ActionCondense is emitted immediately
2. Within PostCommit (lines 611-646):
   - `condenseAndUpdateState()` saves checkpoint to `entire/checkpoints/v1`
   - Clears `FilesTouched`, resets `StepCount = 0`
   - Records `LastCheckpointID` for amend restoration
   - **CARRY-FORWARD (lines 648-665):** 
     - Detects remaining uncommitted files using content-aware comparison
     - Creates NEW shadow branch at current HEAD for remaining files
     - Sets `FilesTouched` to remaining files, `StepCount = 1`, `CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0`
3. Next commit will get its OWN checkpoint ID (1:1 correspondence)

**Key functions:**
- `condenseAndUpdateState()` (lines 704-757) - handles condensation and state update
- `carryForwardToNewShadowBranch()` (lines 1782-1839) - creates new shadow branch with remaining files

---

### 4. SHADOW BRANCH "PULL FORWARD" MECHANISM

**Three key operations work together:**

#### A. Shadow Branch Migration (manual_commit_migration.go)
When HEAD changes (rebase/pull/etc.) mid-session:
- `migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded()` (lines 21-78) renames shadow branch from old commit hash to new commit hash
- Example: `entire/<old-hash7>-<worktreeID6>` → `entire/<new-hash7>-<worktreeID6>`
- Used in `SaveChanges()` and `SaveTaskCheckpoint()` before writing new checkpoints

#### B. Carry-Forward (lines 1782-1839)
After condensation in PostCommit:
```go
if condensed {
    remainingFiles := filesWithRemainingAgentChanges(...)
    if len(remainingFiles) > 0 {
        s.carryForwardToNewShadowBranch(...)
    }
}
```
- Preserves uncommitted files on NEW shadow branch at current HEAD
- Resets `CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0` so next checkpoint is self-contained
- Next commit gets own checkpoint ID

#### C. Clean-up (lines 679-697)
Shadow branches only deleted when ALL sessions are non-active OR were condensed:
```go
for shadowBranchName := range shadowBranchesToDelete {
    if uncondensedActiveOnBranch[shadowBranchName] {
        continue  // Preserve if active session still using it
    }
    deleteShadowBranch(repo, shadowBranchName)
}
```

---

### 5. SAVECHANGES / SAVETASKCHECKPOINT FLOW

**SaveChanges (manual_commit_git.go, lines 23-163):**
1. Load session state
2. **Migration Check:** `migrateAndPersistIfNeeded()` - pulls shadow branch forward if HEAD changed
3. Check if shadow branch exists (for reporting "created" vs "updated")
4. Use **prompt attribution calculated at session start** (PendingPromptAttribution from InitializeSession)
5. Call `store.WriteTemporary()` to create checkpoint on shadow branch
6. Update state: `StepCount++`, append to `PromptAttributions`, merge `FilesTouched`
7. Save state

**SaveTaskCheckpoint (lines 167-290):**
- Same pattern but calls `store.WriteTemporaryTask()` instead
- For subagent work spawned via Task tool
- Formats commit message differently for task checkpoints

**Key insight:** Both use the same migration check - this is how shadow branches are "pulled forward" automatically when HEAD changes.

---

### 6. STOP HOOK / HANDLETURNEND (lines 1583-1723)

**Called when agent turn ends (Stop hook):**
```go
func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) HandleTurnEnd(state *SessionState) error
```

**Job: Finalize mid-turn commits with full transcript**
- During turn, PostCommit writes provisional transcript (whatever was available at commit time)
- HandleTurnEnd reads FULL transcript and replaces provisional with complete
- Iterates over `TurnCheckpointIDs` accumulated during turn
- Calls `store.UpdateCommitted()` to replace provisional transcript

**Key tracking:**
- `state.TurnCheckpointIDs` populated during PostCommit (line 645): tracks all checkpoints created mid-turn
- `state.CheckpointTranscriptStart` tracks where this checkpoint starts in the transcript for incremental updates

---

### 7. SESSION STATE STRUCTURE

**Key fields (session/session.go, aliased in manual_commit_types.go:28-31):**
```go
type SessionState = session.State

// Critical fields for 1:1 checkpoint model:
Phase                    session.Phase        // ACTIVE, IDLE, or ENDED
BaseCommit              string               // Base commit hash (updated on migration)
StepCount              int                   // Number of checkpoints in THIS session
TurnCheckpointIDs      []string              // Checkpoints created mid-turn (for finalization)
CheckpointTranscriptStart int                // Offset where this checkpoint starts (for incremental updates)
LastCheckpointID       checkpoint.ID         // Used by amend operations to restore trailer
FilesTouched           []string              // Files modified in this session
PendingPromptAttribution *PromptAttribution   // Attribution calculated at prompt start
PromptAttributions     []PromptAttribution    // Accumulated attributions per checkpoint
```

---

### 8. KEY BEHAVIORAL CHANGES

| Aspect | Old Model (ACTIVE_COMMITTED) | New Model (1:1 Checkpoints) |
|--------|-----|-----|
| **Mid-turn commit** | ACTIVE → ACTIVE_COMMITTED, defer condensation | ACTIVE, condense NOW in PostCommit |
| **Remaining files** | Lost after condensation | Carried forward to new shadow branch at HEAD |
| **Next commit** | Goes to old shadow branch | Gets new checkpoint ID (1:1 correspondence) |
| **Shadow branch naming** | Based on base commit | Renamed if HEAD changes (migration) |
| **Transcript finalization** | On TurnEnd (after all commits) | On TurnEnd (replaces provisional) |

---

### 9. CRITICAL INTEGRATION POINTS

**PrepareCommitMsg Hook (lines 218-373):**
- Fast path: if agent is active (no TTY), just add trailer without content detection
- Checks `sessionHasNewContent()` to determine if trailer is needed
- Can analyze **live transcript** mid-turn to detect agent changes (lines 909-1029)

**PostCommit Hook (lines 454-700):**
- Runs state machine: ACTIVE + EventGitCommit → ActionCondense
- Executes condensation and carry-forward
- Tracks `TurnCheckpointIDs` for HandleTurnEnd
- Cleans up shadow branches

**InitializeSession (lines 1340-1425):**
- Calculates `PendingPromptAttribution` at prompt start (BEFORE agent runs)
- Initializes session or updates existing
- Migrates shadow branch if HEAD changed

---

### 10. SUBAGENT-ONLY SESSIONS (from ENT-297)

The test `test: add integration test for subagent-only checkpoint creation` (commit 402686e8) shows:
- When an agent spawns a subagent (Task tool), the subagent creates its own checkpoints
- Subagent checkpoints are tracked separately from main session
- HandleTurnEnd finalizes subagent checkpoints alongside main checkpoints
- This required tracking `TurnCheckpointIDs` separately from main session state

---

This architecture enables:
1. **1:1 checkpoint-to-commit correspondence** - each user commit gets exactly one checkpoint ID
2. **Automatic shadow branch migration** when HEAD changes mid-session
3. **Content-aware carry-forward** of partial commits to new shadow branches
4. **Immediate condensation** on PostCommit, not deferred until TurnEnd
5. **Clean session lifecycle** with ACTIVE/IDLE/ENDED phases

2. **`sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript()`** — used by PrepareCommitMsg fast path. Our fix needs to work with the new flow.
3. **Stop hook / HandleTurnEnd** — finalizes provisional transcripts. Subagent files need to be accounted for.

    16→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode"
    17→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
    18→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    19→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session"
    20→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy"
    21→)
    22→
    23→// hookInputData contains parsed hook input and session identifiers.
    24→type hookInputData struct {
    25→	agent     agent.Agent
    26→	input     *agent.HookInput
    27→	sessionID string
    28→}
    29→
    30→// parseAndLogHookInput parses the hook input and sets up logging context.
    31→func parseAndLogHookInput() (*hookInputData, error) {
    32→	// Get the agent from the hook command context (e.g., "entire hooks claude-code ...")
    33→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
    34→	if err != nil {
    35→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
    36→	}
    37→
    38→	// Parse hook input using agent interface
    39→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookUserPromptSubmit, os.Stdin)
    40→	if err != nil {
    41→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
    42→	}
    43→
    44→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
    45→	logging.Info(logCtx, "user-prompt-submit",
    46→		slog.String("hook", "user-prompt-submit"),
    47→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
    48→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
    49→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
    50→	)
    51→
    52→	sessionID := input.SessionID
    53→	if sessionID == "" {
    54→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	// Get the Entire session ID, preferring the persisted value to handle midnight boundary
    58→	return &hookInputData{
    59→		agent:     ag,
    60→		input:     input,
    61→		sessionID: sessionID,
    62→	}, nil
    63→}
    64→
    65→// captureInitialState captures the initial state on user prompt submit.
    66→func captureInitialState() error {
    67→	// Parse hook input and setup logging
    68→	hookData, err := parseAndLogHookInput()
    69→	if err != nil {
    70→		return err
    71→	}
    72→
    73→	// CLI captures state directly (including transcript position)
    74→	if err := CapturePrePromptState(hookData.sessionID, hookData.input.SessionRef); err != nil {
    75→		return err
    76→	}
    77→
    78→	// If strategy implements SessionInitializer, call it to initialize session state
    79→	strat := GetStrategy()
    80→
    81→	// Ensure strategy setup is in place (git hooks, gitignore, metadata branch).
    82→	// Done here at turn start so hooks are installed before any mid-turn commits.
    83→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
    84→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	if initializer, ok := strat.(strategy.SessionInitializer); ok {
    88→		agentType := hookData.agent.Type()
    89→		if err := initializer.InitializeSession(hookData.sessionID, agentType, hookData.input.SessionRef, hookData.input.UserPrompt); err != nil {
    90→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to initialize session state: %v\n", err)
    91→		}
    92→	}
    93→
    94→	return nil
    95→}
    96→
    97→// commitWithMetadata commits the session changes with metadata.
    98→func commitWithMetadata() error { //nolint:maintidx // already present in codebase
    99→	// Get the agent for hook input parsing and session ID transformation
   100→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   101→	if err != nil {
   102→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   103→	}
   104→
   105→	// Parse hook input using agent interface
   106→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookStop, os.Stdin)
   107→	if err != nil {
   108→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   109→	}
   110→
   111→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   112→	logging.Info(logCtx, "stop",
   113→		slog.String("hook", "stop"),
   114→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   115→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   116→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
   117→	)
   118→
   119→	sessionID := input.SessionID
   120→	if sessionID == "" {
   121→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
   122→	}
   123→
   124→	transcriptPath := input.SessionRef
   125→	if transcriptPath == "" || !fileExists(transcriptPath) {
   126→		return fmt.Errorf("transcript file not found or empty: %s", transcriptPath)
   127→	}
   128→
   129→	// Early check: bail out quickly if the repo has no commits yet.
   130→	// Without this, the function does a lot of work (transcript copy, prompt extraction, etc.)
   131→	// before eventually failing deep in strategy.SaveChanges().
   132→	if repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository(); err == nil && strategy.IsEmptyRepository(repo) {
   133→		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping checkpoint. Will activate after first commit.")
   134→		return NewSilentError(strategy.ErrEmptyRepository)
   135→	}
   136→
   137→	// Create session metadata folder using the entire session ID (preserves original date on resume)
   138→	// Use AbsPath to ensure we create at repo root, not relative to cwd
   139→	sessionDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   140→	sessionDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(sessionDir)
   141→	if err != nil {
   142→		sessionDirAbs = sessionDir // Fallback to relative
   143→	}
   144→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   145→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session directory: %w", err)
   146→	}
   147→
   148→	// Wait for Claude Code to flush the transcript file.
   149→	// The stop hook fires before the transcript is fully written to disk.
   150→	// We poll for our own hook_progress sentinel entry in the file tail,
   151→	// which guarantees all prior entries have been flushed.
   152→	waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath, time.Now())
   153→
   154→	// Copy transcript
   155→	logFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   156→	if err := copyFile(transcriptPath, logFile); err != nil {
   157→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to copy transcript: %w", err)
   158→	}
   159→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Copied transcript to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.TranscriptFileName)
   160→
   161→	// Load pre-prompt state (captured on UserPromptSubmit)
   162→	// Needed for transcript offset and file change detection
   163→	preState, err := LoadPrePromptState(sessionID)
   164→	if err != nil {
   165→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   166→	}
   167→
   168→	// Determine transcript offset: prefer pre-prompt state, fall back to session state.
   169→	// Pre-prompt state has the offset when the transcript path was available at prompt time.
   170→	// Session state has the offset updated after each successful checkpoint save (auto-commit).
   171→	var transcriptOffset int
   172→	if preState != nil && preState.StepTranscriptStart > 0 {
   173→		transcriptOffset = preState.StepTranscriptStart
   174→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state found: parsing transcript from line %d\n", transcriptOffset)
   175→	} else {
   176→		// Fall back to session state (e.g., auto-commit strategy updates it after each save)
   177→		sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   178→		if loadErr != nil {
   179→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   180→		}
   181→		if sessionState != nil && sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart > 0 {
   182→			transcriptOffset = sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart
   183→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session state found: parsing transcript from line %d\n", transcriptOffset)
   184→		}
   185→	}
   186→
   187→	// Parse transcript (optionally from offset for strategies that track transcript position)
   188→	// When transcriptOffset > 0, only parse NEW lines since the last checkpoint
   189→	var transcript []transcriptLine
   190→	var totalLines int
   191→	if transcriptOffset > 0 {
   192→		// Parse only NEW lines since last checkpoint
   193→		transcript, totalLines, err = parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, transcriptOffset)
   194→		if err != nil {
   195→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript from line %d: %w", transcriptOffset, err)
   196→		}
   197→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Parsed %d new transcript lines (total: %d)\n", len(transcript), totalLines)
   198→	} else {
   199→		// First prompt or no session state - parse entire transcript
   200→		// Use parseTranscriptFromLine with offset 0 to also get totalLines
   201→		transcript, totalLines, err = parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, 0)
   202→		if err != nil {
   203→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   204→		}
   205→	}
   206→
   207→	// Extract all prompts since last checkpoint for prompt file
   208→	allPrompts := extractUserPrompts(transcript)
   209→	promptFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.PromptFileName)
   210→	promptContent := strings.Join(allPrompts, "\n\n---\n\n")
   211→	if err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(promptContent), 0o600); err != nil {
   212→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write prompt file: %w", err)
   213→	}
   214→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted %d prompt(s) to: %s\n", len(allPrompts), sessionDir+"/"+paths.PromptFileName)
   215→
   216→	// Extract summary
   217→	summaryFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.SummaryFileName)
   218→	summary := extractLastAssistantMessage(transcript)
   219→	if err := os.WriteFile(summaryFile, []byte(summary), 0o600); err != nil {
   220→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write summary file: %w", err)
   221→	}
   222→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted summary to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.SummaryFileName)
   223→
   224→	// Get modified files from transcript (main agent + subagents).
   225→	// Subagent transcripts live in <transcriptDir>/<modelSessionID>/subagents/
   226→	subagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(transcriptPath), input.SessionID, "subagents")
   227→	modifiedFiles, subagentErr := claudecode.ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptPath, transcriptOffset, subagentsDir)
   228→	if subagentErr != nil {
   229→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files with subagents: %v\n", subagentErr)
   230→		// Fall back to main transcript only
   231→		modifiedFiles = extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   232→	}
   233→
   234→	// Generate commit message from last user prompt
   235→	lastPrompt := ""
   236→	if len(allPrompts) > 0 {
   237→		lastPrompt = allPrompts[len(allPrompts)-1]
   238→	}
   239→	commitMessage := generateCommitMessage(lastPrompt)
   240→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Using commit message: %s\n", commitMessage)
   241→
   242→	// Get repo root for path conversion (not cwd, since Claude may be in a subdirectory)
   243→	// Using cwd would filter out files in sibling directories (paths starting with ..)
   244→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   245→	if err != nil {
   246→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   247→	}
   248→
   249→	if preState != nil {
   250→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state: %d pre-existing untracked files\n", len(preState.UntrackedFiles))
   251→	}
   252→
   253→	// Compute new and deleted files (single git status call)
   254→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())
   255→	if err != nil {
   256→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   257→	}
   258→
   259→	// Filter and normalize all paths (CLI responsibility)
   260→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   261→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   262→	if changes != nil {
   263→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   264→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   265→	}
   266→
   267→	// Check if there are any changes to commit
   268→	totalChanges := len(relModifiedFiles) + len(relNewFiles) + len(relDeletedFiles)
   269→	if totalChanges == 0 {
   270→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No files were modified during this session\n")
   271→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipping commit\n")
   272→		// Still transition phase even when skipping commit — the turn is ending.
   273→		transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   274→		// Clean up state even when skipping
   275→		if err := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); err != nil {
   276→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   277→		}
   278→		return nil
   279→	}
   280→
   281→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files modified during session (%d):\n", len(relModifiedFiles))
   282→	for _, file := range relModifiedFiles {
   283→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   284→	}
   285→	if len(relNewFiles) > 0 {
   286→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "New files created (%d):\n", len(relNewFiles))
   287→		for _, file := range relNewFiles {
   288→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  + %s\n", file)
   289→		}
   290→	}
   291→	if len(relDeletedFiles) > 0 {
   292→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files deleted (%d):\n", len(relDeletedFiles))
   293→		for _, file := range relDeletedFiles {
   294→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   295→		}
   296→	}
   297→
   298→	// Create context file before saving changes
   299→	contextFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.ContextFileName)
   300→	if err := createContextFileMinimal(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID, promptFile, summaryFile, transcript); err != nil {
   301→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create context file: %w", err)
   302→	}
   303→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created context file: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.ContextFileName)
   304→
   305→	// Get git author from local/global config
   306→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   307→	if err != nil {
   308→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   309→	}
   310→
   311→	// Get the configured strategy
   312→	strat := GetStrategy()
   313→
   314→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   315→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   316→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   317→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   318→	}
   319→
   320→	// Get transcript position from pre-prompt state (captured at step/turn start)
   321→	var transcriptIdentifierAtStart string
   322→	var transcriptLinesAtStart int
   323→	if preState != nil {
   324→		transcriptIdentifierAtStart = preState.LastTranscriptIdentifier
   325→		transcriptLinesAtStart = preState.StepTranscriptStart
   326→	}
   327→
   328→	// Calculate token usage for this checkpoint (Claude Code specific)
   329→	var tokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage
   330→	if transcriptPath != "" {
   331→		usage, tokenErr := claudecode.CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptPath, transcriptLinesAtStart, subagentsDir)
   332→		if tokenErr != nil {
   333→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage: %v\n", tokenErr)
   334→		} else {
   335→			tokenUsage = usage
   336→		}
   337→	}
   338→
   339→	// Build fully-populated save context and delegate to strategy
   340→	ctx := strategy.SaveContext{
   341→		SessionID:                sessionID,
   342→		ModifiedFiles:            relModifiedFiles,
   343→		NewFiles:                 relNewFiles,
   344→		DeletedFiles:             relDeletedFiles,
   345→		MetadataDir:              sessionDir,
   346→		MetadataDirAbs:           sessionDirAbs,
   347→		CommitMessage:            commitMessage,
   348→		TranscriptPath:           transcriptPath,
   349→		AuthorName:               author.Name,
   350→		AuthorEmail:              author.Email,
   351→		AgentType:                agentType,
   352→		StepTranscriptIdentifier: transcriptIdentifierAtStart,
   353→		StepTranscriptStart:      transcriptLinesAtStart,
   354→		TokenUsage:               tokenUsage,
   355→	}
   356→
   357→	if err := strat.SaveChanges(ctx); err != nil {
   358→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save changes: %w", err)
   359→	}
   360→
   361→	// Update session state with new transcript position for strategies that create
   362→	// commits on the active branch (auto-commit strategy). This prevents parsing old transcript
   363→	// lines on subsequent checkpoints.
   364→	// Note: Shadow strategy tracks transcript position per-step via StepTranscriptStart in
   365→	// pre-prompt state, but doesn't advance CheckpointTranscriptStart in session state because
   366→	// its checkpoints accumulate all files touched across the entire session.
   367→	if strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit {
   368→		// Load session state for updating transcript position
   369→		sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   370→		if loadErr != nil {
   371→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   372→		}
   373→		// Create session state lazily if it doesn't exist (backward compat for resumed sessions
   374→		// or if InitializeSession was never called/failed)
   375→		if sessionState == nil {
   376→			sessionState = &strategy.SessionState{
   377→				SessionID: sessionID,
   378→			}
   379→		}
   380→		sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = totalLines
   381→		sessionState.StepCount++
   382→		if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); updateErr != nil {
   383→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", updateErr)
   384→		} else {
   385→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Updated session state: transcript position=%d, checkpoint=%d\n",
   386→				totalLines, sessionState.StepCount)
   387→		}
   388→	}
   389→
   390→	// Fire EventTurnEnd to transition session phase (all strategies).
   391→	// This moves ACTIVE → IDLE.
   392→	transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   393→
   394→	// Clean up pre-prompt state (CLI responsibility)
   395→	if err := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); err != nil {
   396→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   397→	}
   398→
   399→	return nil
   400→}
   401→
   402→// handleClaudeCodePostTodo handles the PostToolUse[TodoWrite] hook for subagent checkpoints.
   403→// Creates a checkpoint if we're in a subagent context (active pre-task file exists).
   404→// Skips silently if not in subagent context (main agent).
   405→func handleClaudeCodePostTodo() error {
   406→	input, err := parseSubagentCheckpointHookInput(os.Stdin)
   407→	if err != nil {
   408→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[TodoWrite] input: %w", err)
   409→	}
   410→
   411→	// Get agent for logging context
   412→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   413→	if err != nil {
   414→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   415→	}
   416→
   417→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   418→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-todo",
   419→		slog.String("hook", "post-todo"),
   420→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   421→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   422→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
   423→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   424→	)
   425→
   426→	// Check if we're in a subagent context by looking for an active pre-task file
   427→	taskToolUseID, found := FindActivePreTaskFile()
   428→	if !found {
   429→		// Not in subagent context - this is a main agent TodoWrite, skip
   430→		return nil
   431→	}
   432→
   433→	// Skip on default branch to avoid polluting main/master history
   434→	if skip, branchName := ShouldSkipOnDefaultBranch(); skip {
   435→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping incremental checkpoint on branch '%s'\n", branchName)
   436→		return nil
   437→	}
   438→
   439→	// Detect file changes since last checkpoint
   440→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(nil)
   441→	if err != nil {
   442→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to detect changed files: %v\n", err)
   443→		return nil
   444→	}
   445→
   446→	// If no file changes, skip creating a checkpoint
   447→	if len(changes.Modified) == 0 && len(changes.New) == 0 && len(changes.Deleted) == 0 {
   448→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping incremental checkpoint\n")
   449→		return nil
   450→	}
   451→
   452→	// Get git author
   453→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   454→	if err != nil {
   455→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to get git author: %v\n", err)
   456→		return nil
   457→	}
   458→
   459→	// Get the active strategy
   460→	strat := GetStrategy()
   461→
   462→	// Get the session ID from the transcript path or input, then transform to Entire session ID
   463→	sessionID := input.SessionID
   464→	if sessionID == "" {
   465→		sessionID = paths.ExtractSessionIDFromTranscriptPath(input.TranscriptPath)
   466→	}
   467→
   468→	// Get next checkpoint sequence
   469→	seq := GetNextCheckpointSequence(sessionID, taskToolUseID)
   470→
   471→	// Extract the todo content from the tool_input.
   472→	// PostToolUse receives the NEW todo list where the just-completed work is
   473→	// marked as "completed". The last completed item is the work that was just done.
   474→	todoContent := ExtractLastCompletedTodoFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   475→	if todoContent == "" {
   476→		// No completed items - this is likely the first TodoWrite (planning phase).
   477→		// Check if there are any todos at all to avoid duplicate messages.
   478→		todoCount := CountTodosFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   479→		if todoCount > 0 {
   480→			// Use "Planning: N todos" format for the first TodoWrite
   481→			todoContent = fmt.Sprintf("Planning: %d todos", todoCount)
   482→		}
   483→		// If todoCount == 0, todoContent remains empty and FormatIncrementalMessage
   484→		// will fall back to "Checkpoint #N" format
   485→	}
   486→
   487→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   488→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   489→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   490→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   491→	}
   492→
   493→	// Build incremental checkpoint context
   494→	ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
   495→		SessionID:           sessionID,
   496→		ToolUseID:           taskToolUseID,
   497→		ModifiedFiles:       changes.Modified,
   498→		NewFiles:            changes.New,
   499→		DeletedFiles:        changes.Deleted,
   500→		TranscriptPath:      input.TranscriptPath,
   501→		AuthorName:          author.Name,
   502→		AuthorEmail:         author.Email,
   503→		IsIncremental:       true,
   504→		IncrementalSequence: seq,
   505→		IncrementalType:     input.ToolName,
   506→		IncrementalData:     input.ToolInput,
   507→		TodoContent:         todoContent,
   508→		AgentType:           agentType,
   509→	}
   510→
   511→	// Save incremental checkpoint
   512→	if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   513→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to save incremental checkpoint: %v\n", err)
   514→		return nil
   515→	}
   516→
   517→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Created incremental checkpoint #%d for %s (task: %s)\n",
   518→		seq, input.ToolName, taskToolUseID[:min(12, len(taskToolUseID))])
   519→	return nil
   520→}
   521→
   522→// handleClaudeCodePreTask handles the PreToolUse[Task] hook
   523→func handleClaudeCodePreTask() error {
   524→	input, err := parseTaskHookInput(os.Stdin)
   525→	if err != nil {
   526→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PreToolUse[Task] input: %w", err)
   527→	}
   528→
   529→	// Get agent for logging context
   530→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   531→	if err != nil {
   532→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   533→	}
   534→
   535→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   536→	logging.Info(logCtx, "pre-task",
   537→		slog.String("hook", "pre-task"),
   538→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   539→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   540→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
   541→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   542→	)
   543→
   544→	// Log context to stdout
   545→	logPreTaskHookContext(os.Stdout, input)
   546→
   547→	// Capture pre-task state locally (for computing new files when task completes).
   548→	// We don't create a shadow branch commit here. Commits are created during
   549→	// task completion (handleClaudeCodePostTask/handleClaudeCodePostTodo) only if the task resulted
   550→	// in file changes.
   551→	if err := CapturePreTaskState(input.ToolUseID); err != nil {
   552→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to capture pre-task state: %w", err)
   553→	}
   554→
   555→	return nil
   556→}
   557→
   558→// handleClaudeCodePostTask handles the PostToolUse[Task] hook
   559→func handleClaudeCodePostTask() error {
   560→	input, err := parsePostTaskHookInput(os.Stdin)
   561→	if err != nil {
   562→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[Task] input: %w", err)
   563→	}
   564→
   565→	// Extract subagent type from tool_input for logging
   566→	subagentType, taskDescription := ParseSubagentTypeAndDescription(input.ToolInput)
   567→
   568→	// Get agent for logging context
   569→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   570→	if err != nil {
   571→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   572→	}
   573→
   574→	// Log parsed input context
   575→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   576→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-task",
   577→		slog.String("hook", "post-task"),
   578→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   579→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   580→		slog.String("agent_id", input.AgentID),
   581→		slog.String("subagent_type", subagentType),
   582→	)
   583→
   584→	// Determine subagent transcript path
   585→	transcriptDir := filepath.Dir(input.TranscriptPath)
   586→	var subagentTranscriptPath string
   587→	if input.AgentID != "" {
   588→		subagentTranscriptPath = AgentTranscriptPath(transcriptDir, input.AgentID)
   589→		if !fileExists(subagentTranscriptPath) {
   590→			subagentTranscriptPath = ""
   591→		}
   592→	}
   593→
   594→	// Log context to stdout
   595→	logPostTaskHookContext(os.Stdout, input, subagentTranscriptPath)
   596→
   597→	// Parse transcript to extract modified files
   598→	var modifiedFiles []string
   599→	if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   600→		// Use subagent transcript if available
   601→		transcript, err := parseTranscript(subagentTranscriptPath)
   602→		if err != nil {
   603→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to parse subagent transcript: %v\n", err)
   604→		} else {
   605→			modifiedFiles = extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   606→		}
   607→	} else {
   608→		// Fall back to main transcript
   609→		transcript, err := parseTranscript(input.TranscriptPath)
   610→		if err != nil {
   611→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to parse transcript: %v\n", err)
   612→		} else {
   613→			modifiedFiles = extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   614→		}
   615→	}
   616→
   617→	// Load pre-task state and compute new files
   618→	preState, err := LoadPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID)
   619→	if err != nil {
   620→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-task state: %v\n", err)
   621→	}
   622→
   623→	// Compute new and deleted files (single git status call)
   624→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())
   625→	if err != nil {
   626→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   627→	}
   628→
   629→	// Get repo root for path conversion (not cwd, since Claude may be in a subdirectory)
   630→	// Using cwd would filter out files in sibling directories (paths starting with ..)
   631→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   632→	if err != nil {
   633→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   634→	}
   635→
   636→	// Filter and normalize paths
   637→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   638→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   639→	if changes != nil {
   640→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   641→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   642→	}
   643→
   644→	// If no file changes, skip creating a checkpoint
   645→	if len(relModifiedFiles) == 0 && len(relNewFiles) == 0 && len(relDeletedFiles) == 0 {
   646→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping task checkpoint\n")
   647→		// Cleanup pre-task state (ignore error - cleanup is best-effort)
   648→		_ = CleanupPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   649→		return nil
   650→	}
   651→
   652→	// Find checkpoint UUID from main transcript (best-effort, ignore errors)
   653→	transcript, _ := parseTranscript(input.TranscriptPath) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort extraction
   654→	checkpointUUID, _ := FindCheckpointUUID(transcript, input.ToolUseID)
   655→
   656→	// Get git author
   657→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   658→	if err != nil {
   659→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   660→	}
   661→
   662→	// Get the configured strategy
   663→	strat := GetStrategy()
   664→
   665→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   666→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   667→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   668→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   669→	}
   670→
   671→	// Build task checkpoint context - strategy handles metadata creation
   672→	// Note: Incremental checkpoints are now created during task execution via handleClaudeCodePostTodo,
   673→	// so we don't need to collect/cleanup staging area here.
   674→	ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
   675→		SessionID:              input.SessionID,
   676→		ToolUseID:              input.ToolUseID,
   677→		AgentID:                input.AgentID,
   678→		ModifiedFiles:          relModifiedFiles,
   679→		NewFiles:               relNewFiles,
   680→		DeletedFiles:           relDeletedFiles,
   681→		TranscriptPath:         input.TranscriptPath,
   682→		SubagentTranscriptPath: subagentTranscriptPath,
   683→		CheckpointUUID:         checkpointUUID,
   684→		AuthorName:             author.Name,
   685→		AuthorEmail:            author.Email,
   686→		SubagentType:           subagentType,
   687→		TaskDescription:        taskDescription,
   688→		AgentType:              agentType,
   689→	}
   690→
   691→	// Call strategy to save task checkpoint - strategy handles all metadata creation
   692→	if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   693→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save task checkpoint: %w", err)
   694→	}
   695→
   696→	// Cleanup pre-task state (ignore error - cleanup is best-effort)
   697→	_ = CleanupPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   698→
   699→	return nil
   700→}
   701→
   702→// handleClaudeCodeSessionStart handles the SessionStart hook for Claude Code.
   703→func handleClaudeCodeSessionStart() error {
   704→	return handleSessionStartCommon()
   705→}
   706→
   707→// handleClaudeCodeSessionEnd handles the SessionEnd hook for Claude Code.
   708→// This fires when the user explicitly closes the session.
   709→// Updates the session state with EndedAt timestamp.
   710→func handleClaudeCodeSessionEnd() error {
   711→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   712→	if err != nil {
   713→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   714→	}
   715→
   716→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookSessionEnd, os.Stdin)
   717→	if err != nil {
   718→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   719→	}
   720→
   721→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   722→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-end",
   723→		slog.String("hook", "session-end"),
   724→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   725→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   726→	)
   727→
   728→	if input.SessionID == "" {
   729→		return nil // No session to update
   730→	}
   731→
   732→	// Best-effort cleanup - don't block session closure on failure
   733→	if err := markSessionEnded(input.SessionID); err != nil {
   734→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to mark session ended: %v\n", err)
   735→	}
   736→	return nil
   737→}
   738→
   739→// transitionSessionTurnEnd fires EventTurnEnd to move the session from
   740→// ACTIVE → IDLE. Best-effort: logs warnings on failure rather than
   741→// returning errors.
   742→func transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID string) {
   743→	turnState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   744→	if loadErr != nil {
   745→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for turn end: %v\n", loadErr)
   746→		return
   747→	}
   748→	if turnState == nil {
   749→		return
   750→	}
   751→	strategy.TransitionAndLog(turnState, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
   752→
   753→	// Always dispatch to strategy for turn-end handling. The strategy reads
   754→	// work items from state (e.g. TurnCheckpointIDs), not the action list.
   755→	strat := GetStrategy()
   756→	if handler, ok := strat.(strategy.TurnEndHandler); ok {
   757→		if err := handler.HandleTurnEnd(turnState); err != nil {
   758→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end action dispatch failed: %v\n", err)
   759→		}
   760→	}
   761→
   762→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(turnState); updateErr != nil {
   763→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session phase on turn end: %v\n", updateErr)
   764→	}
   765→}
   766→
   767→// markSessionEnded transitions the session to ENDED phase via the state machine.
   768→func markSessionEnded(sessionID string) error {
   769→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   770→	if err != nil {
   771→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   772→	}
   773→	if state == nil {
   774→		return nil // No state file, nothing to update
   775→	}
   776→
   777→	strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStop, session.TransitionContext{})
   778→
   779→	now := time.Now()
   780→	state.EndedAt = &now
   781→
   782→	if err := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   783→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   784→	}
   785→	return nil
   786→}
   787→
   788→// stopHookSentinel is the string that appears in Claude Code's hook_progress
   789→// transcript entry when it launches our stop hook. Used to detect that the
   790→// transcript file has been fully flushed before we copy it.
   791→const stopHookSentinel = "hooks claude-code stop"
   792→
   793→// waitForTranscriptFlush polls the transcript file tail for the hook_progress
   794→// sentinel entry that Claude Code writes when launching the stop hook.
   795→// Once this entry appears in the file, all prior entries (assistant replies,
   796→// tool results) are guaranteed to have been flushed.
   797→//
   798→// hookStartTime is the approximate time our process started, used to avoid
   799→// matching stale sentinel entries from previous stop hook invocations.
   800→//
   801→// Falls back silently after a timeout — the transcript copy will proceed
   802→// with whatever data is available.
   803→func waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath string, hookStartTime time.Time) {
   804→	const (
   805→		maxWait      = 3 * time.Second
   806→		pollInterval = 50 * time.Millisecond
   807→		tailBytes    = 4096 // Read last 4KB — sentinel is near the end
   808→		maxSkew      = 2 * time.Second
   809→	)
   810→
   811→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks")
   812→	deadline := time.Now().Add(maxWait)
   813→	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
   814→		if checkStopSentinel(transcriptPath, tailBytes, hookStartTime, maxSkew) {
   815→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel found",
   816→				slog.Duration("wait", time.Since(hookStartTime)),
   817→			)
   818→			return
   819→		}
   820→		time.Sleep(pollInterval)
   821→	}
   822→	// Timeout — proceed with whatever is on disk.
   823→	logging.Warn(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel not found within timeout, proceeding",
   824→		slog.Duration("timeout", maxWait),
   825→	)
   826→}
   827→
   828→// checkStopSentinel reads the tail of the transcript file and looks for a
   829→// hook_progress entry containing the stop hook sentinel, with a timestamp
   830→// close to hookStartTime.
   831→func checkStopSentinel(path string, tailBytes int64, hookStartTime time.Time, maxSkew time.Duration) bool {
   832→	f, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path comes from agent hook input
   833→	if err != nil {
   834→		return false
   835→	}
   836→	defer f.Close()
   837→
   838→	// Seek to tail
   839→	info, err := f.Stat()
   840→	if err != nil {
   841→		return false
   842→	}
   843→	offset := info.Size() - tailBytes
   844→	if offset < 0 {
   845→		offset = 0
   846→	}
   847→	buf := make([]byte, info.Size()-offset)
   848→	if _, err := f.ReadAt(buf, offset); err != nil {
   849→		return false
   850→	}
   851→
   852→	// Scan lines from the tail for the sentinel
   853→	lines := strings.Split(string(buf), "\n")
   854→	for _, line := range lines {
   855→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   856→		if line == "" || !strings.Contains(line, stopHookSentinel) {
   857→			continue
   858→		}
   859→
   860→		// Parse timestamp to check recency
   861→		var entry struct {
   862→			Timestamp string `json:"timestamp"`
   863→		}
   864→		if json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry) != nil || entry.Timestamp == "" {
   865→			continue
   866→		}
   867→		ts, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, entry.Timestamp)
   868→		if err != nil {
   869→			ts, err = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, entry.Timestamp)
   870→			if err != nil {
   871→				continue
   872→			}
   873→		}
   874→
   875→		// Check timestamp is within skew window of our start time
   876→		if ts.After(hookStartTime.Add(-maxSkew)) && ts.Before(hookStartTime.Add(maxSkew)) {
   877→			return true
   878→		}
   879→	}
   880→	return false
   881→}
   882→

   817→// filterSessionsWithNewContent returns sessions that have new transcript content
   818→// beyond what was already condensed.
   819→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) filterSessionsWithNewContent(repo *git.Repository, sessions []*SessionState) []*SessionState {
   820→	var result []*SessionState
   821→
   822→	for _, state := range sessions {
   823→		hasNew, err := s.sessionHasNewContent(repo, state)
   824→		if err != nil {
   825→			// On error, include the session (fail open for hooks)
   826→			result = append(result, state)
   827→			continue
   828→		}
   829→		if hasNew {
   830→			result = append(result, state)
   831→		}
   832→	}
   833→
   834→	return result
   835→}
   836→
   837→// sessionHasNewContent checks if a session has new transcript content
   838→// beyond what was already condensed.
   839→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) sessionHasNewContent(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) (bool, error) {
   840→	// Get shadow branch
   841→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   842→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   843→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   844→	if err != nil {
   845→		// No shadow branch means no Stop has happened since the last condensation.
   846→		// However, the agent may have done work (including commits) without a Stop.
   847→		// Check the live transcript to detect this scenario.
   848→		return s.sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo, state)
   849→	}
   850→
   851→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   852→	if err != nil {
   853→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
   854→	}
   855→
   856→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   857→	if err != nil {
   858→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
   859→	}
   860→
   861→	// Look for transcript file
   862→	metadataDir := paths.EntireMetadataDir + "/" + state.SessionID
   863→	var transcriptLines int
   864→	var hasTranscriptFile bool
   865→
   866→	if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileName); fileErr == nil {
   867→		hasTranscriptFile = true
   868→		if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   869→			transcriptLines = countTranscriptItems(state.AgentType, content)
   870→		}
   871→	} else if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy); fileErr == nil {
   872→		hasTranscriptFile = true
   873→		if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   874→			transcriptLines = countTranscriptItems(state.AgentType, content)
   875→		}
   876→	}
   877→
   878→	// If shadow branch exists but has no transcript (e.g., carry-forward from mid-session commit),
   879→	// check if the session has FilesTouched. Carry-forward sets FilesTouched with remaining files.
   880→	if !hasTranscriptFile {
   881→		if len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 {
   882→			// Shadow branch has files from carry-forward - check if staged files overlap
   883→			// AND have matching content (content-aware check).
   884→			stagedFiles := getStagedFiles(repo)
   885→			result := stagedFilesOverlapWithContent(repo, tree, stagedFiles, state.FilesTouched)
   886→			return result, nil
   887→		}
   888→		// No transcript and no FilesTouched - fall back to live transcript check
   889→		return s.sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo, state)
   890→	}
   891→
   892→	// Has new content if there are more lines than already condensed
   893→	return transcriptLines > state.CheckpointTranscriptStart, nil
   894→}
   895→
   896→// sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript checks if a session has new content
   897→// by examining the live transcript file. This is used when no shadow branch exists
   898→// (i.e., no Stop has happened yet) but the agent may have done work.
   899→//
   900→// Returns true if:
   901→//  1. The transcript has grown since the last condensation, AND
   902→//  2. The new transcript portion contains file modifications, AND
   903→//  3. At least one modified file overlaps with the currently staged files
   904→//
   905→// The overlap check ensures we don't add checkpoint trailers to commits that are
   906→// unrelated to the agent's recent changes.
   907→//
   908→// This handles the scenario where the agent commits mid-session before Stop.
   909→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) (bool, error) {
   910→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   911→
   912→	// Need both transcript path and agent type to analyze
   913→	if state.TranscriptPath == "" || state.AgentType == "" {
   914→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "live transcript check: missing transcript path or agent type",
   915→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   916→			slog.String("transcript_path", state.TranscriptPath),
   917→			slog.String("agent_type", string(state.AgentType)),
   918→		)
   919→		return false, nil
   920→	}
   921→
   922→	// Get the agent for transcript analysis
   923→	ag, err := agent.GetByAgentType(state.AgentType)
   924→	if err != nil {
   925→		return false, nil //nolint:nilerr // Unknown agent type, fail gracefully
   926→	}
   927→
   928→	// Cast to TranscriptAnalyzer
   929→	analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer)

   896→// sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript checks if a session has new content
   897→// by examining the live transcript file. This is used when no shadow branch exists
   898→// (i.e., no Stop has happened yet) but the agent may have done work.
   899→//
   900→// Returns true if:
   901→//  1. The transcript has grown since the last condensation, AND
   902→//  2. The new transcript portion contains file modifications, AND
   903→//  3. At least one modified file overlaps with the currently staged files
   904→//
   905→// The overlap check ensures we don't add checkpoint trailers to commits that are
   906→// unrelated to the agent's recent changes.
   907→//
   908→// This handles the scenario where the agent commits mid-session before Stop.
   909→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) (bool, error) {
   910→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   911→
   912→	// Need both transcript path and agent type to analyze
   913→	if state.TranscriptPath == "" || state.AgentType == "" {
   914→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "live transcript check: missing transcript path or agent type",
   915→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   916→			slog.String("transcript_path", state.TranscriptPath),
   917→			slog.String("agent_type", string(state.AgentType)),
   918→		)
   919→		return false, nil
   920→	}
   921→
   922→	// Get the agent for transcript analysis
   923→	ag, err := agent.GetByAgentType(state.AgentType)
   924→	if err != nil {
   925→		return false, nil //nolint:nilerr // Unknown agent type, fail gracefully
   926→	}
   927→
   928→	// Cast to TranscriptAnalyzer
   929→	analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer)
   930→	if !ok {
   931→		return false, nil // Agent doesn't support transcript analysis
   932→	}
   933→
   934→	// Get current transcript position
   935→	currentPos, err := analyzer.GetTranscriptPosition(state.TranscriptPath)
   936→	if err != nil {
   937→		return false, nil //nolint:nilerr // Error reading transcript, fail gracefully
   938→	}
   939→
   940→	// Check if transcript has grown since last condensation
   941→	if currentPos <= state.CheckpointTranscriptStart {
   942→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "live transcript check: no new content",
   943→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   944→			slog.Int("current_pos", currentPos),
   945→			slog.Int("start_offset", state.CheckpointTranscriptStart),
   946→		)
   947→		return false, nil // No new content
   948→	}
   949→
   950→	// Transcript has grown - check if there are file modifications in the new portion
   951→	modifiedFiles, _, err := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(state.TranscriptPath, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   952→	if err != nil {
   953→		return false, nil //nolint:nilerr // Error parsing transcript, fail gracefully
   954→	}
   955→
   956→	// Also check subagent transcripts for file modifications (Claude Code specific).
   957→	// Subagents spawned via the Task tool write to separate transcript files that
   958→	// the main transcript analyzer doesn't see.
   959→	if state.AgentType == agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode && state.TranscriptPath != "" {
   960→		subagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(state.TranscriptPath), state.SessionID, "subagents")
   961→		allFiles, extractErr := claudecode.ExtractAllModifiedFiles(
   962→			state.TranscriptPath, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart, subagentsDir,
   963→		)
   964→		if extractErr != nil {
   965→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "live transcript check: failed to extract subagent modified files",
   966→				slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   967→				slog.String("error", extractErr.Error()),
   968→			)
   969→		} else if len(allFiles) > len(modifiedFiles) {
   970→			modifiedFiles = allFiles
   971→		}
   972→	}
   973→
   974→	// No file modifications means no new content to checkpoint
   975→	if len(modifiedFiles) == 0 {
   976→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "live transcript check: transcript grew but no file modifications",
   977→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   978→		)
   979→		return false, nil
   980→	}
   981→
   982→	// Normalize modified files from absolute to repo-relative paths.
   983→	// Transcript tool_use entries contain absolute paths (e.g., /Users/alex/project/src/main.go)
   984→	// but getStagedFiles returns repo-relative paths (e.g., src/main.go).
   985→	// Use state.WorktreePath (already resolved) to avoid an extra git subprocess.
   986→	basePath := state.WorktreePath
   987→	if basePath == "" {
   988→		if wp, wpErr := GetWorktreePath(); wpErr == nil {
   989→			basePath = wp
   990→		}
   991→	}
   992→	if basePath != "" {
   993→		normalized := make([]string, 0, len(modifiedFiles))
   994→		for _, f := range modifiedFiles {
   995→			if rel := paths.ToRelativePath(f, basePath); rel != "" {
   996→				normalized = append(normalized, rel)
   997→			} else {
   998→				// Already relative or outside repo — keep as-is
   999→				normalized = append(normalized, f)
  1000→			}
  1001→		}
  1002→		modifiedFiles = normalized
  1003→	}
  1004→
  1005→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "live transcript check: found file modifications",
  1006→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1007→		slog.Int("modified_files", len(modifiedFiles)),
  1008→	)
  1009→
  1010→	// Check if any modified files overlap with currently staged files
  1011→	// This ensures we only add checkpoint trailers to commits that include
  1012→	// files the agent actually modified
  1013→	stagedFiles := getStagedFiles(repo)
  1014→
  1015→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "live transcript check: comparing staged vs modified",
  1016→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1017→		slog.Int("staged_files", len(stagedFiles)),
  1018→		slog.Int("modified_files", len(modifiedFiles)),
  1019→	)
  1020→
  1021→	if !hasOverlappingFiles(stagedFiles, modifiedFiles) {
  1022→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "live transcript check: no overlap between staged and modified files",
  1023→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1024→		)
  1025→		return false, nil // No overlap - staged files are unrelated to agent's work
  1026→	}
  1027→
  1028→	return true, nil
  1029→}

  1031→// sessionHasNewContentInCommittedFiles checks if a session has content that overlaps with
  1032→// the committed files. This is used in PostCommit for ACTIVE sessions where staged files
  1033→// are empty (already committed). Uses the live transcript to extract modified files and
  1034→// compares against the committed file set.
  1035→//
  1036→// KNOWN LIMITATION: This function relies on transcript analysis to detect file modifications.
  1037→// If the agent makes file modifications via shell commands (e.g., `sed`, `mv`, `cp`) that
  1038→// aren't captured in the transcript's file modification tracking, those modifications may
  1039→// not be detected. This is an acceptable edge case because:
  1040→// 1. Most agent file modifications use the Write/Edit tools which are tracked
  1041→// 2. Shell-based modifications are relatively rare in practice
  1042→// 3. The consequence (missing a checkpoint trailer) is minor - the transcript is still saved
  1043→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) sessionHasNewContentInCommittedFiles(state *SessionState, committedFiles map[string]struct{}) bool {
  1044→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
  1045→
  1046→	// Need both transcript path and agent type to analyze
  1047→	if state.TranscriptPath == "" || state.AgentType == "" {
  1048→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "committed files check: missing transcript path or agent type",
  1049→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1050→			slog.String("transcript_path", state.TranscriptPath),
  1051→			slog.String("agent_type", string(state.AgentType)),
  1052→		)
  1053→		return false
  1054→	}

   575→		// For mid-session commits (ACTIVE, no shadow branch), state.FilesTouched may be empty
   576→		// because no SaveChanges/Stop has been called yet. Extract files from transcript.
   577→		filesTouchedBefore := make([]string, len(state.FilesTouched))

   591→		condensed := false
   592→
   593→		// Dispatch strategy-specific actions.
   594→		// Each branch handles its own BaseCommit update so there is no
   595→		// fallthrough conditional at the end. On condensation failure,
   596→		// BaseCommit is intentionally NOT updated to preserve access to
   597→		// the shadow branch (which is named after the old BaseCommit).
   598→		for _, action := range remaining {
   599→			switch action {
   600→			case session.ActionCondense:
   601→				// For ACTIVE sessions, any commit during the turn is session-related.
   602→				// For IDLE/ENDED sessions (e.g., carry-forward), also require that the
   603→				// committed files overlap with the session's remaining files AND have
   604→				// matching content — otherwise an unrelated commit (or a commit with
   605→				// completely replaced content) would incorrectly get this session's checkpoint.
   606→				shouldCondense := hasNew
   607→				if shouldCondense && !state.Phase.IsActive() {
   608→					shouldCondense = filesOverlapWithContent(repo, shadowBranchName, commit, state.FilesTouched)
   609→				}
   610→				if shouldCondense {
   611→					condensed = s.condenseAndUpdateState(logCtx, repo, checkpointID, state, head, shadowBranchName, shadowBranchesToDelete)
   612→					// condenseAndUpdateState updates BaseCommit on success.
   613→					// On failure, BaseCommit is preserved so the shadow branch remains accessible.
   614→				} else {
   615→					// No new content or unrelated commit — just update BaseCommit
   616→					s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx, state, newHead)
   617→				}
   618→			case session.ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched:

   624→					// On failure, BaseCommit is preserved (same as ActionCondense).
   625→				} else {
   626→					s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx, state, newHead)
   627→				}
   628→			case session.ActionDiscardIfNoFiles:
   629→				if len(state.FilesTouched) == 0 {
   630→					logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: skipping empty ended session (no files to condense)",
   631→						slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   632→					)
   633→				}
   634→				s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx, state, newHead)
   635→			case session.ActionClearEndedAt, session.ActionUpdateLastInteraction:
   636→				// Handled by session.ApplyCommonActions above
   637→			case session.ActionWarnStaleSession:
   638→				// Not produced by EventGitCommit; listed for switch exhaustiveness
   639→			}
   640→		}
   641→
   642→		// Record checkpoint ID for ACTIVE sessions so HandleTurnEnd can finalize
   643→		// with full transcript. IDLE/ENDED sessions already have complete transcripts.
   644→		if condensed && state.Phase.IsActive() {

   646→		}

   651→		// Uses content-aware comparison: if user did `git add -p` and committed

   660→				slog.Any("remaining", remainingFiles),
   661→			)

   664→			}
   665→		}

   670→		}

   673→		// Condensed sessions already have their data on entire/checkpoints/v1.
   674→		if state.Phase.IsActive() && !condensed {
   675→			uncondensedActiveOnBranch[shadowBranchName] = true
   676→		}
   677→	}

   680→	// or were condensed during this PostCommit.
   681→	for shadowBranchName := range shadowBranchesToDelete {

   690→		} else {
   691→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Cleaned up shadow branch: %s\n", shadowBranchName)
   692→			logging.Info(logCtx, "shadow branch deleted",
   693→				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   694→				slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   695→			)
   696→		}
   697→	}

   700→}

  1036→// KNOWN LIMITATION: This function relies on transcript analysis to detect file modifications.
  1037→// If the agent makes file modifications via shell commands (e.g., `sed`, `mv`, `cp`) that

> AGENT

Here's what I found. There are **two functions** that are missing subagent file awareness:

### Gap 1: `extractFilesFromLiveTranscript()` (line 1140)

Called at line 580 during PostCommit carry-forward prep when `state.FilesTouched` is empty during a mid-turn commit. It only calls `analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset()` on the main transcript — subagent files are invisible. This means `filesTouchedBefore` will be incomplete, and `filesWithRemainingAgentChanges()` won't know about subagent files, so carry-forward will **lose them**.

### Gap 2: `sessionHasNewContentInCommittedFiles()` (line 1043)

Called at line 555 in PostCommit for ACTIVE sessions with `StepCount == 0 && len(FilesTouched) == 0`. Same problem — only checks the main transcript. If only subagents modified files, this returns false and the commit **doesn't get a checkpoint**.

### What's already correct

- `sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript()` (line 909) — **already fixed** by our commits, includes subagent check
- Stop hook `commitWithMetadata()` — **already fixed**, calls `ExtractAllModifiedFiles()`

Both gaps follow the exact same pattern as the fix we already applied to `sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript`. Want me to fix them?

All tests pass — unit and integration. Here's a summary of the two fixes:

**`extractFilesFromLiveTranscript()`** — Added subagent transcript check after the main transcript extraction. Uses offset 0 (matching the existing behavior of extracting ALL files for carry-forward). This ensures subagent-modified files are included in `filesTouchedBefore`, so carry-forward correctly preserves them across mid-turn commits.

**`sessionHasNewContentInCommittedFiles()`** — Added subagent transcript check after the main transcript extraction. Uses `state.CheckpointTranscriptStart` (matching the existing behavior for incremental detection). This ensures PostCommit recognizes commits containing subagent-modified files for ACTIVE sessions with no prior checkpoints.

Both follow the exact same pattern already established in `sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript()`.

At least one modified file overlaps with the currently staged files\n//\n// The overlap check ensures we don't add checkpoint trailers to commits that are\n// unrelated to the agent's recent changes.\n//\n// This handles the scenario where the agent commits mid-session before Stop.\nfunc (s *ManualCommitStrategy) sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) (bool, error) {\n\tlogCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), \"checkpoint\")\n\n\t// Need both transcript path and agent type to analyze\n\tif state.TranscriptPath == \"\" || state.AgentType == \"\" {\n\t\tlogging.Debug(logCtx, \"live transcript check: missing transcript path or agent type\",\n\t\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", false, nil // No new content\n\t}\n\n\t// Transcript has grown - check if there are file modifications in the new portion\n\tmodifiedFiles, _, err := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(state.TranscriptPath, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, nil //nolint:nilerr // Error parsing transcript, fail gracefully\n\t}\n\n\t// Also check subagent transcripts for file modifications (Claude Code specific).\n\t// Subagents spawned via the Task tool write to separate transcript files that\n\t// the main transcript analyzer doesn't see.\n\tif state.AgentType == agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode && state.TranscriptPath != \"\" {\n\t\tsubagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(state.TranscriptPath), != nil {\n\t\t\tlogging.Debug(logCtx, \"live transcript check: failed to extract subagent modified files\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", state.SessionID),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"error\", extractErr.Error()),\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t} else if len(allFiles) > len(modifiedFiles) {\n\t\t\tmodifiedFiles = allFiles\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// No file modifications means no new content to checkpoint\n\tif len(modifiedFiles) == 0 {\n\t\tlogging.Debug(logCtx, \"live transcript check: transcript grew but no file modifications\",\n\t\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", state.SessionID),\n\t\t)\n\t\treturn false, nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Normalize modified files from absolute to repo-relative paths.\n\t// Transcript tool_use entries contain absolute paths (e.g., /Users/alex/project/src/main.go)\n\t// but \"\" {\n\t\tif wp, wpErr := GetWorktreePath(); wpErr == nil {\n\t\t\tbasePath = wp\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif basePath != \"\" {\n\t\tnormalized := make([]string, 0, len(modifiedFiles))\n\t\tfor _, f := range modifiedFiles {\n\t\t\tif rel := paths.ToRelativePath(f, basePath); rel != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tnormalized = append(normalized, rel)\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t// Already relative or outside repo — keep as-is\n\t\t\t\tnormalized = append(normalized, f)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tmodifiedFiles = normalized\n\t}\n\n\tlogging.Debug(logCtx, \"live transcript check: found file modifications\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", state.SessionID),\n\t\tslog.Int(\"modified_files\", len(modifiedFiles)),\n\t)\n\n\t// Check if any modified files overlap with currently staged files\n\t// This ensures we only add checkpoint trailers to commits that include\n\t// files the agent actually modified\n\tstagedFiles := getStagedFiles(repo)\n\n\tlogging.Debug(logCtx, \"live transcript check: comparing staged vs modified\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", state.SessionID),\n\t\tslog.Int(\"staged_files\", len(stagedFiles)),\n\t\tslog.Int(\"modified_files\", len(modifiedFiles)),\n\t)\n\n\tif !hasOverlappingFiles(stagedFiles, modifiedFiles) {\n\t\tlogging.Debug(logCtx, \"live transcript check: no overlap between staged and modified files\",\n\t\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", state.SessionID),\n\t\t)\n\t\treturn false, nil // No overlap - staged files are unrelated to agent's work\n\t}\n\n\treturn true, nil\n}",
  "new_string": "// sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript checks if a session has new content\n// by examining the live transcript file. This is used when no shadow branch exists\n// (i.e., no Stop has happened yet) but the agent may have done work.\n//\n// Returns true if:\n//  1. The transcript has grown since the last condensation, AND\n//  2. The new transcript portion contains file modifications, AND\n//  3. At least one modified file overlaps with the currently staged files\n//\n// The overlap check ensures we don't add checkpoint trailers to commits that are\n// unrelated to the agent's recent changes.\n//\n// This handles the scenario where the agent commits mid-session before Stop.\nfunc (s *ManualCommitStrategy) sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) (bool, error) {\n\tlogCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), \"checkpoint\")\n\n\tmodifiedFiles, ok := s.extractNewModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript(state)\n\tif !ok || len(modifiedFiles) == 0 {\n\t\treturn false, nil\n\t}\n\n\tlogging.Debug(logCtx, \"live transcript check: found file modifications\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", state.SessionID),\n\t\tslog.Int(\"modified_files\", len(modifiedFiles)),\n\t)\n\n\t// Check if any modified files overlap with currently staged files\n\t// This ensures we only add checkpoint trailers to commits that include\n\t// files the agent actually modified\n\tstagedFiles := getStagedFiles(repo)\n\n\tlogging.Debug(logCtx, \"live transcript check: comparing staged vs modified\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", state.SessionID),\n\t\tslog.Int(\"staged_files\", len(stagedFiles)),\n\t\tslog.Int(\"modified_files\", len(modifiedFiles)),\n\t)\n\n\tif !hasOverlappingFiles(stagedFiles, modifiedFiles) {\n\t\tlogging.Debug(logCtx, \"live transcript check: no overlap between staged and modified files\",\n\t\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", state.SessionID),\n\t\t)\n\t\treturn false, nil // No overlap - staged files are unrelated to agent's work\n\t}\n\n\treturn true, nil\n}"
}

   982→// extractFilesFromLiveTranscript extracts modified file paths from the live transcript.

   985→// computation which needs to know all files touched, not just new ones.

   993→// and sessionHasNewContentInCommittedFiles to detect agent work.
   994→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractNewModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript(state *SessionState) ([]string, bool) {

> AGENT

All tests pass (unit + integration + race detection). Here's what the refactor did:

**Before:** The subagent extraction + path normalization pattern was copy-pasted in 3 functions.

**After:** One shared helper `extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript(state, offset)` that handles:
- Agent lookup + TranscriptAnalyzer cast
- Main transcript extraction
- Subagent transcript augmentation (Claude Code specific)
- Path normalization to repo-relative

The three callers are now thin wrappers:
- **`extractFilesFromLiveTranscript`** — calls helper with offset 0 (carry-forward needs all files)
- **`extractNewModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript`** — adds transcript growth check, then calls helper with `CheckpointTranscriptStart`
- Both `sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript` and `sessionHasNewContentInCommittedFiles` use `extractNewModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript` and just differ in what they compare against (staged files vs committed files)

The `logInvoked` is logged at DEBUG level, so it wouldn't appear in the user's INFO-level logs. That explains why there's no PostCommit entry. But the key question is: did PostCommit actually run and what happened inside it?

Let me look at the PostCommit entry point to understand the early-exit conditions.
